✅ Statewide Starting Points (Start Here)

  • NJ 211 (Primary “Start Here”) — Dial 211 or 1-877-652-1148 • Text ZIP to 898-211 • nj211.org
  • NJ Department of Human Services (DHS) — nj.gov/humanservices • 222 South Warren Street, PO Box 700, Trenton, NJ 08625-0700
  • NJ Housing & Mortgage Finance Agency (NJHMFA) — nj.gov/dca/hmfa • Phone: 609-278-7400 • 637 South Clinton Avenue, Trenton, NJ 08611
  • NJ Department of Children and Families (DCF) — nj.gov/dcf • Phone: 1-855-463-6323 • 50 East State Street, 2nd floor, PO Box 729, Trenton, NJ 08625-0729

🤖 AI Skills (Free/Low‑Cost) — Training + Practice + Platforms

Want AI skills without a big budget? Start with free foundations, practice in a browser notebook, then try tools for writing, art, video, and audio. Cost note: most platforms have free tiers with limits; higher tiers can get pricey—always check cost details before subscribing.

🎓 Free/Low‑Cost Classes (Beginner → Job‑Ready)

  • Elements of AI — beginner-friendly intro (no math/programming required).
  • Google Machine Learning Crash Course — practical ML basics + exercises.
  • Microsoft Learn: AI Fundamentals — short modules (includes responsible AI).
  • Kaggle Learn — bite‑size courses + datasets to practice.
  • freeCodeCamp: Machine Learning with Python — structured practice (free).
  • Generative AI for Beginners (Microsoft) — lessons + hands‑on projects.
  • fast.ai: Practical Deep Learning — great if you have some coding experience.
  • AWS Skill Builder (Digital Training) — many free courses; paid options exist.

🧪 Practice (Free Browser Labs)

  • Google Colab — run Python notebooks in your browser (free options; paid upgrades exist).
  • Kaggle Notebooks — free notebooks + datasets + community examples.

🧰 AI Platforms to Try (Writing • Coding • Art • Video • Audio)

  • Chat & writing: ChatGPT • Microsoft Copilot • Google Gemini
  • Coding/building: Replit AI • GitHub Copilot (limited free tier available)
  • Images & art: Bing Image Creator • Microsoft Designer • Ideogram • Canva Magic Media • Adobe Firefly • Krea • Leonardo AI
  • Video: Runway • CapCut
  • Music & voice: Suno • ElevenLabs
  • Try many tools in one place: Hugging Face Spaces — directory of free AI demos you can run in a browser.

Privacy & safety: Don’t share passwords, SSN, bank info, or private legal/medical details in any AI chat. Double‑check important info and cite sources when using AI for school/work.

🤖 AI Skills (Free/Low‑Cost) — Training + Practice + Platforms

Want AI skills without a big budget? Start with free foundations, practice in a browser notebook, then try tools for writing, art, video, and audio. Cost note: most platforms have free tiers with limits; higher tiers can get pricey—always check cost details before subscribing.

🎓 Free/Low‑Cost Classes (Beginner → Job‑Ready)

  • Elements of AI — beginner-friendly intro (no math/programming required).
  • Google Machine Learning Crash Course — practical ML basics + exercises.
  • Microsoft Learn: AI Fundamentals — short modules (includes responsible AI).
  • Kaggle Learn — bite‑size courses + datasets to practice.
  • freeCodeCamp: Machine Learning with Python — structured practice (free).
  • Generative AI for Beginners (Microsoft) — lessons + hands‑on projects.
  • fast.ai: Practical Deep Learning — great if you have some coding experience.
  • AWS Skill Builder (Digital Training) — many free courses; paid options exist.

🧪 Practice (Free Browser Labs)

  • Google Colab — run Python notebooks in your browser (free options; paid upgrades exist).
  • Kaggle Notebooks — free notebooks + datasets + community examples.

🧰 AI Platforms to Try (Writing • Coding • Art • Video • Audio)

  • Chat & writing: ChatGPT • Microsoft Copilot • Google Gemini
  • Coding/building: Replit AI • GitHub Copilot (limited free tier available)
  • Images & art: Bing Image Creator • Microsoft Designer • Ideogram • Canva Magic Media • Adobe Firefly • Krea • Leonardo AI
  • Video: Runway • CapCut
  • Music & voice: Suno • ElevenLabs
  • Try many tools in one place: Hugging Face Spaces — directory of free AI demos you can run in a browser.

Privacy & safety: Don’t share passwords, SSN, bank info, or private legal/medical details in any AI chat. Double‑check important info and cite sources when using AI for school/work.

🤖 AI Skills (Free/Low‑Cost) — Training + Practice + Platforms

Want AI skills without a big budget? Start with free foundations, practice in a browser notebook, then try tools for writing, art, video, and audio. Cost note: most platforms have free tiers with limits; higher tiers can get pricey—always check cost details before subscribing.

🎓 Free/Low‑Cost Classes (Beginner → Job‑Ready)

  • Elements of AI — beginner-friendly intro (no math/programming required).
  • Google Machine Learning Crash Course — practical ML basics + exercises.
  • Microsoft Learn: AI Fundamentals — short modules (includes responsible AI).
  • Kaggle Learn — bite‑size courses + datasets to practice.
  • freeCodeCamp: Machine Learning with Python — structured practice (free).
  • Generative AI for Beginners (Microsoft) — lessons + hands‑on projects.
  • fast.ai: Practical Deep Learning — great if you have some coding experience.
  • AWS Skill Builder (Digital Training) — many free courses; paid options exist.

🧪 Practice (Free Browser Labs)

  • Google Colab — run Python notebooks in your browser (free options; paid upgrades exist).
  • Kaggle Notebooks — free notebooks + datasets + community examples.

🧰 AI Platforms to Try (Writing • Coding • Art • Video • Audio)

  • Chat & writing: ChatGPT • Microsoft Copilot • Google Gemini
  • Coding/building: Replit AI • GitHub Copilot (limited free tier available)
  • Images & art: Bing Image Creator • Microsoft Designer • Ideogram • Canva Magic Media • Adobe Firefly • Krea • Leonardo AI
  • Video: Runway • CapCut
  • Music & voice: Suno • ElevenLabs
  • Try many tools in one place: Hugging Face Spaces — directory of free AI demos you can run in a browser.

Privacy & safety: Don’t share passwords, SSN, bank info, or private legal/medical details in any AI chat. Double‑check important info and cite sources when using AI for school/work.

  • 🏛️ New Jersey DHS — nj.gov/humanservices (programs, benefits, crisis & supports).
  • 🏙️ Elizabeth / Union County — ucnj.org and elizabethnj.org (local departments & contacts).
  • 🧭 Local Tip: If you’re not sure where to start, begin with Family Promise of Union County and ask for referrals to programs closest to your ZIP.

🤖 AI Skills (Free/Low‑Cost) — Training + Practice + Platforms

Want AI skills without a big budget? Start with free foundations, practice in a browser notebook, then try tools for writing, art, video, and audio. Cost note: most platforms have free tiers with limits; higher tiers can get pricey—always check cost details before subscribing.

🎓 Free/Low‑Cost Classes (Beginner → Job‑Ready)

  • Elements of AI — beginner-friendly intro (no math/programming required).
  • Google Machine Learning Crash Course — practical ML basics + exercises.
  • Microsoft Learn: AI Fundamentals — short modules (includes responsible AI).
  • Kaggle Learn — bite‑size courses + datasets to practice.
  • freeCodeCamp: Machine Learning with Python — structured practice (free).
  • Generative AI for Beginners (Microsoft) — lessons + hands‑on projects.
  • fast.ai: Practical Deep Learning — great if you have some coding experience.
  • AWS Skill Builder (Digital Training) — many free courses; paid options exist.

🧪 Practice (Free Browser Labs)

  • Google Colab — run Python notebooks in your browser (free options; paid upgrades exist).
  • Kaggle Notebooks — free notebooks + datasets + community examples.

🧰 AI Platforms to Try (Writing • Coding • Art • Video • Audio)

  • Chat & writing: ChatGPT • Microsoft Copilot • Google Gemini
  • Coding/building: Replit AI • GitHub Copilot (limited free tier available)
  • Images & art: Bing Image Creator • Microsoft Designer • Ideogram • Canva Magic Media • Adobe Firefly • Krea • Leonardo AI
  • Video: Runway • CapCut
  • Music & voice: Suno • ElevenLabs
  • Try many tools in one place: Hugging Face Spaces — directory of free AI demos you can run in a browser.

Privacy & safety: Don’t share passwords, SSN, bank info, or private legal/medical details in any AI chat. Double‑check important info and cite sources when using AI for school/work.

🤖 AI Skills (Free/Low‑Cost) — Training + Practice + Platforms

Want AI skills without a big budget? Start with free foundations, practice in a browser notebook, then try tools for writing, art, video, and audio. Cost note: most platforms have free tiers with limits; higher tiers can get pricey—always check cost details before subscribing.

🎓 Free/Low‑Cost Classes (Beginner → Job‑Ready)

  • Elements of AI — beginner-friendly intro (no math/programming required).
  • Google Machine Learning Crash Course — practical ML basics + exercises.
  • Microsoft Learn: AI Fundamentals — short modules (includes responsible AI).
  • Kaggle Learn — bite‑size courses + datasets to practice.
  • freeCodeCamp: Machine Learning with Python — structured practice (free).
  • Generative AI for Beginners (Microsoft) — lessons + hands‑on projects.
  • fast.ai: Practical Deep Learning — great if you have some coding experience.
  • AWS Skill Builder (Digital Training) — many free courses; paid options exist.

🧪 Practice (Free Browser Labs)

  • Google Colab — run Python notebooks in your browser (free options; paid upgrades exist).
  • Kaggle Notebooks — free notebooks + datasets + community examples.

🧰 AI Platforms to Try (Writing • Coding • Art • Video • Audio)

  • Chat & writing: ChatGPT • Microsoft Copilot • Google Gemini
  • Coding/building: Replit AI • GitHub Copilot (limited free tier available)
  • Images & art: Bing Image Creator • Microsoft Designer • Ideogram • Canva Magic Media • Adobe Firefly • Krea • Leonardo AI
  • Video: Runway • CapCut
  • Music & voice: Suno • ElevenLabs
  • Try many tools in one place: Hugging Face Spaces — directory of free AI demos you can run in a browser.

Privacy & safety: Don’t share passwords, SSN, bank info, or private legal/medical details in any AI chat. Double‑check important info and cite sources when using AI for school/work.

🤖 AI Skills (Free/Low‑Cost) — Training + Practice + Platforms

Want AI skills without a big budget? Start with free foundations, practice in a browser notebook, then try tools for writing, art, video, and audio. Cost note: most platforms have free tiers with limits; higher tiers can get pricey—always check cost details before subscribing.

🎓 Free/Low‑Cost Classes (Beginner → Job‑Ready)

  • Elements of AI — beginner-friendly intro (no math/programming required).
  • Google Machine Learning Crash Course — practical ML basics + exercises.
  • Microsoft Learn: AI Fundamentals — short modules (includes responsible AI).
  • Kaggle Learn — bite‑size courses + datasets to practice.
  • freeCodeCamp: Machine Learning with Python — structured practice (free).
  • Generative AI for Beginners (Microsoft) — lessons + hands‑on projects.
  • fast.ai: Practical Deep Learning — great if you have some coding experience.
  • AWS Skill Builder (Digital Training) — many free courses; paid options exist.

🧪 Practice (Free Browser Labs)

  • Google Colab — run Python notebooks in your browser (free options; paid upgrades exist).
  • Kaggle Notebooks — free notebooks + datasets + community examples.

🧰 AI Platforms to Try (Writing • Coding • Art • Video • Audio)

  • Chat & writing: ChatGPT • Microsoft Copilot • Google Gemini
  • Coding/building: Replit AI • GitHub Copilot (limited free tier available)
  • Images & art: Bing Image Creator • Microsoft Designer • Ideogram • Canva Magic Media • Adobe Firefly • Krea • Leonardo AI
  • Video: Runway • CapCut
  • Music & voice: Suno • ElevenLabs
  • Try many tools in one place: Hugging Face Spaces — directory of free AI demos you can run in a browser.

Privacy & safety: Don’t share passwords, SSN, bank info, or private legal/medical details in any AI chat. Double‑check important info and cite sources when using AI for school/work.

🤖 AI Skills (Free/Low‑Cost) — Training + Practice + Platforms

Want AI skills without a big budget? Start with free foundations, practice in a browser notebook, then try tools for writing, art, video, and audio. Cost note: most platforms have free tiers with limits; higher tiers can get pricey—always check cost details before subscribing.

🎓 Free/Low‑Cost Classes (Beginner → Job‑Ready)

  • Elements of AI — beginner-friendly intro (no math/programming required).
  • Google Machine Learning Crash Course — practical ML basics + exercises.
  • Microsoft Learn: AI Fundamentals — short modules (includes responsible AI).
  • Kaggle Learn — bite‑size courses + datasets to practice.
  • freeCodeCamp: Machine Learning with Python — structured practice (free).
  • Generative AI for Beginners (Microsoft) — lessons + hands‑on projects.
  • fast.ai: Practical Deep Learning — great if you have some coding experience.
  • AWS Skill Builder (Digital Training) — many free courses; paid options exist.

🧪 Practice (Free Browser Labs)

  • Google Colab — run Python notebooks in your browser (free options; paid upgrades exist).
  • Kaggle Notebooks — free notebooks + datasets + community examples.

🧰 AI Platforms to Try (Writing • Coding • Art • Video • Audio)

  • Chat & writing: ChatGPT • Microsoft Copilot • Google Gemini
  • Coding/building: Replit AI • GitHub Copilot (limited free tier available)
  • Images & art: Bing Image Creator • Microsoft Designer • Ideogram • Canva Magic Media • Adobe Firefly • Krea • Leonardo AI
  • Video: Runway • CapCut
  • Music & voice: Suno • ElevenLabs
  • Try many tools in one place: Hugging Face Spaces — directory of free AI demos you can run in a browser.

Privacy & safety: Don’t share passwords, SSN, bank info, or private legal/medical details in any AI chat. Double‑check important info and cite sources when using AI for school/work.

🤖 AI Skills (Free/Low‑Cost) — Training + Practice + Platforms

Want AI skills without a big budget? Start with free foundations, practice in a browser notebook, then try tools for writing, art, video, and audio. Cost note: most platforms have free tiers with limits; higher tiers can get pricey—always check cost details before subscribing.

🎓 Free/Low‑Cost Classes (Beginner → Job‑Ready)

  • Elements of AI — beginner-friendly intro (no math/programming required).
  • Google Machine Learning Crash Course — practical ML basics + exercises.
  • Microsoft Learn: AI Fundamentals — short modules (includes responsible AI).
  • Kaggle Learn — bite‑size courses + datasets to practice.
  • freeCodeCamp: Machine Learning with Python — structured practice (free).
  • Generative AI for Beginners (Microsoft) — lessons + hands‑on projects.
  • fast.ai: Practical Deep Learning — great if you have some coding experience.
  • AWS Skill Builder (Digital Training) — many free courses; paid options exist.

🧪 Practice (Free Browser Labs)

  • Google Colab — run Python notebooks in your browser (free options; paid upgrades exist).
  • Kaggle Notebooks — free notebooks + datasets + community examples.

🧰 AI Platforms to Try (Writing • Coding • Art • Video • Audio)

  • Chat & writing: ChatGPT • Microsoft Copilot • Google Gemini
  • Coding/building: Replit AI • GitHub Copilot (limited free tier available)
  • Images & art: Bing Image Creator • Microsoft Designer • Ideogram • Canva Magic Media • Adobe Firefly • Krea • Leonardo AI
  • Video: Runway • CapCut
  • Music & voice: Suno • ElevenLabs
  • Try many tools in one place: Hugging Face Spaces — directory of free AI demos you can run in a browser.

Privacy & safety: Don’t share passwords, SSN, bank info, or private legal/medical details in any AI chat. Double‑check important info and cite sources when using AI for school/work.

  • All‑in‑one demo library: Hugging Face Spaces

🧭 Life Stages, Benefits, Insurance & Future Planning Help

For children and family benefits, disability benefits, Social Security, Medicare, Medicaid/CHIP, health/dental/life insurance, pensions, 401(k), IRA, Roth IRA, survivor benefits, and scam protection, use the national planning guide first, then check this state page for local offices and applications.

Open Life Stages & Benefits Planning

📚 Records, Archives & Family History Help

For birth, death, marriage, divorce, adoption, cemetery/burial, obituary, court, probate, military, immigration/naturalization, property/land, genealogy, archives, historical, and tribal/Indigenous records, start with this state’s vital records office, county clerk, courthouse, probate court, recorder/register of deeds, state archives, local library, cemetery office, or official agency connected to the record.

Open Records & Family History Guide

📘 Education Records, School Transcripts & Credentials

For elementary, middle, high school, GED/HSE, special education records, trade school, college/university transcripts, graduate records, apprenticeships, or closed school records, start with this state’s school, school district, registrar, state education agency, adult education office, higher education agency, licensing agency, or official records custodian.

Open Education Records & Transcript Guide

Personal, Medical, Student Clinic, Legal, Property & Service Records

For hospital, clinic, health department, patient file, student clinic, SOAP note, clinical encounter note, dental chart, shot, legal, court, juvenile, incarceration, mortgage, property, utility, phone, cable, internet, work, training, learner feedback, or other service records, start with the provider, clinic, school/program, agency, court, company, county/city office, employer, or official account that created the record.

Open Personal & Service Records Help

Money, Housing, Benefits & Life Wellness

Use the verified life-help center for official housing, benefits, retirement, consumer, tax, estate-planning, education, veterans, travel, and worldwide starting points.
